C2PA's Content Credentials are a tamper-evident, cryptographically signed digital manifest providing verifiable provenance for digital content such as images, videos, or audio files. The metadata type, according to Adobe , serves as a "digital nutrition label," giving information about the creator, how it was made, and if it was generated using artificial intelligence (AI).
